# Prereg 3, run 2: the reproduction of each qualifying packet

PRE-REGISTRATION-2.md §8, for FOUND: "The write-up is the reproduction." This file is that reproduction for prereg 3's run-2 verdict (`results/prereg3/run2/verdict.json`, LOG §35). It is generated by `ruling/reproduction_run2.py` from committed records only, and holds no file content: each file of each packet is what its command prints.

Qualifying packets: 413. Their answers are in `qualifying.md`.

## Setup

Clone the arm's source and check out its pin (PRE-REGISTRATION-2.md §6.2), or fetch the archived bundle whose sha256 `harness/p2/bundles.json` records. Set `CLONE` to that clone.

| arm | source | pin | bundle sha256 |
|---|---|---|---|
| rust-book | `https://github.com/rust-lang/book` | `1500248d8f230566e4ec9f27fcbb8fe9e2898ab1` | `2dc07598fd32b77124e504d6499c6ff0a54dd1056d3cdf412050dca41e4f1384` |
| obsidian-help | `https://github.com/obsidianmd/obsidian-help` | `327a782e90481268361b5ccccdb0c224b2b13fe6` | `008cbe84af65cc7e6dd1974947bfb8a9f27be54a8641bab882bf44546acb109c` |
| cmspec | `https://github.com/commonmark/commonmark-spec` | `3da939428d80f146f270cd1765e4ba462e96bb1b` | `8bf9a1d0f26233998e7c51268dcba596b678d9e479ee9e5cd32f30d8e85dcef6` |
| k8s-en | `https://github.com/kubernetes/website` | `6b27baef1e44275fd4368e14375296e1dfe5af11` | `86096a651f3f9c5a594f33104f02fa36fcea9b1ff06ad8de4caba9680cfef0ed` |
| k8s-l10n | `https://github.com/kubernetes/website` | `6b27baef1e44275fd4368e14375296e1dfe5af11` | `86096a651f3f9c5a594f33104f02fa36fcea9b1ff06ad8de4caba9680cfef0ed` |
| cncf-toc | `https://github.com/cncf/toc` | `144c2e3215884e498e744cc51e6b7cef82d654f1` | `63515ba0df9e4f78544ec3d7b3fda67f1d66ad565739b3044ecd93a21fcf8a2f` |
| site-policy | `https://github.com/github/site-policy` | `b9578b546d2506febda1da2cd7431644d58e512c` | `bc8d34cb1e7182002fc2b00cc6a360f18b4a2246c1affb17af35aeea271731fb` |

The harness reads every blob as UTF-8 with universal newlines (`p2/arms.py` `decode_text`), and the packet files hold that text. So each command's output is piped through `norm` below. For M, the after-file is the stock-git merge of the two legs on the merge base, as `prose_merge.stock_merge` runs it (PRE-REGISTRATION-2.md §3(1)): a fresh repository, no hooks, no attributes, `git -c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=<empty> merge --no-edit legC`. The corpus's own merge commit is listed for reference; the harness does not use its result.

```sh
norm() {  # UTF-8 with universal newlines, as the harness reads every blob
  python3 -I -S -c 'import sys; t = sys.stdin.buffer.read().decode("utf-8"); sys.stdout.buffer.write(t.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n").encode("utf-8"))'
}
stock_merge() {  # base legA legC path: prints the merged file
  local d f; d=$(mktemp -d); mkdir "$d/.nohooks"; f="$d/$4"; mkdir -p "$(dirname "$f")"
  local G=(git -C "$d" -c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c commit.gpgsign=false -c core.autocrlf=false
           -c merge.conflictStyle=merge -c core.hooksPath="$d/.nohooks")
  export GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM=/dev/null LC_ALL=C \
         GIT_AUTHOR_NAME=h GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL=h@invalid GIT_COMMITTER_NAME=h GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL=h@invalid \
         GIT_AUTHOR_DATE=2000-01-01T00:00:00Z GIT_COMMITTER_DATE=2000-01-01T00:00:00Z
  "${G[@]}" init -q -b main
  git -C "$CLONE" show "$1:$4" | norm > "$f"; "${G[@]}" add -A; "${G[@]}" commit -q -m base
  "${G[@]}" checkout -q -b legA
  git -C "$CLONE" show "$2:$4" | norm > "$f"; "${G[@]}" add -A; "${G[@]}" commit -q --allow-empty -m a
  "${G[@]}" checkout -q -b legC main
  git -C "$CLONE" show "$3:$4" | norm > "$f"; "${G[@]}" add -A; "${G[@]}" commit -q --allow-empty -m c
  "${G[@]}" checkout -q legA
  "${G[@]}" merge --no-edit legC >/dev/null && cat "$f"
}
```

Each packet below gives the record the harness judged, its cells, the commits and path, the commands for each file, and the classes: the oracle's decided verdict and its target block index, and the hardened resolver's class, status and the block index it hit. A qualifying record is a decided hardened mis-resolution (`hard` WRONG) that the tierer placed in tier A or B with q3 and q4 "no". The oracle's target and the resolver's hit index the blocks of the after-file from 0; a hit given as a pair is its first and last block.
